function readCookie(name) {
    var nameEQ = name + "=";
    var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
    for ( var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
        var c = ca[i];
        while (c.charAt(0) == ' ')
            c = c.substring(1, c.length);
        if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0)
            return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
    }
    return null;
}

.ot-fade-in {
  animation-name: none !important;
  animation-duration: initial !important;
  animation-timing-function: initial !important;

  }
}
@media screen and (min-width: 425px) {

                      AMP.setState({ stateHdr: { search: false, searchDesk: false } })
                    } else if (top < lastScrollTop) {
                      AMP.setState({ stateHdr: { search: true, searchDesk: true } })
                    }
                    lastScrollTop = top <= 0 ? 0 : top;
                },false);
            });
        </script>

</span><span class="pron dpron">/<span class="ipa dipa lpr-2 lpl-1">trʌŋk</span>/</span></span></div><div class="pos-body">

                <div class="ddef_h"><span class="def-info ddef-info">  </span><div class="def ddef_d db">A person’s trunk is the <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/main" title="main" rel="">main</a> <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/part" title="part" rel="">part</a> of the <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/body" title="body" rel="">body</a>, not <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/include" title="including" rel="">including</a> the <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/head" title="head" rel="">head</a>, <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/leg" title="legs" rel="">legs</a>, or <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/arms" title="arms" rel="">arms</a>.</div> </div></div></div>           <div class="pb-inline-sense"></div>
       </div>

                        <div class="pr dsense "><div class="cid" id="cacd-1-2"></div> <h3 class="dsense_h"><span class="hw dsense_hw">trunk</span> <span class="pos dsense_pos" title="A word that refers to a person, place, idea, event or thing.">noun</span> <span class="dgram">[C]</span> <span class="guideword dsense_gw" title="Guide word: helps you find the right meaning when a word has more than one meaning">

      </span></h3> <div class="sense-body dsense_b"><div class="def-block ddef_block " data-wl-senseid="CACD_00018857_03">

                        <div class="pr dsense "><div class="cid" id="cacd-1-3"></div> <h3 class="dsense_h"><span class="hw dsense_hw">trunk</span> <span class="pos dsense_pos" title="A word that refers to a person, place, idea, event or thing.">noun</span> <span class="dgram">[C]</span> <span class="guideword dsense_gw" title="Guide word: helps you find the right meaning when a word has more than one meaning">
            (<span>STORAGE SPACE</span>)
      </span></h3> <div class="sense-body dsense_b"><div class="def-block ddef_block " data-wl-senseid="CACD_00018857_04">
                        <div class="dwl hax">
                            
         
        <a amp-access="loggedIn" amp-access-hide="" class="dwla harzsgqwhvasgy7zft3yivy7y7yim533s7" title="Add this meaning to a word list" on="tap:AMP.setState({ stateGlobal: { wlSenseId: 'CACD_00018857_04', wotd: '    trunk
' } }), sidebarWordList.open">

        </a>
        <a amp-access="NOT loggedIn" class="dwla harzsgqwhvasgy7zft3yivy7y7yim533s7" title="Add this meaning to a word list" on="tap:amp-access.login-sign-in">

        </a>
        </div>
                <div class="ddef_h"><span class="def-info ddef-info">  </span><div class="def ddef_d db">a <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/closed" title="closed" rel="">closed</a> <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/space" title="space" rel="">space</a> at the back of a <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/car" title="car" rel="">car</a> where things can be <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/store" title="stored" rel="">stored</a></div> </div></div><div class="def-block ddef_block " data-wl-senseid="CACD_00018857_05">

        <a amp-access="loggedIn" amp-access-hide="" class="dwla harzsgqwhvasgy7zft3yivy7y7yim533s7" title="Add this meaning to a word list" on="tap:AMP.setState({ stateGlobal: { wlSenseId: 'CACD_00018857_05', wotd: '    trunk
' } }), sidebarWordList.open">

        </a>
        <a amp-access="NOT loggedIn" class="dwla harzsgqwhvasgy7zft3yivy7y7yim533s7" title="Add this meaning to a word list" on="tap:amp-access.login-sign-in">

            <i class="i i-list-ul" aria-hidden="true"></i>
        </a>
        </div>
                <div class="ddef_h"><span class="def-info ddef-info">  </span><div class="def ddef_d db">A trunk is also a <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/large" title="large" rel="">large</a>, <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/strong" title="strong" rel="">strong</a> <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/case" title="case" rel="">case</a> used to <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/store" title="store" rel="">store</a> or <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/transport" title="transport" rel="">transport</a> <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/clothes" title="clothes" rel="">clothes</a> and other possessions.</div> </div></div></div>           <div class="pb-inline-sense"></div>
                                                            <div id="harzsgqwhvvy7y0qs73n73tzs3y09" class="am-default_moreslots contentslot" data-harzsgqwhvcsscznyi85ng0yify7="CN-bwKuG15YDFbpIXgQdoVQhEg"><div id="harzsgqwhvcssczny0vy7ty0fxgvjny0yw9k9w92yfoywqy7sy0j15tzs39y09y0y0qs73vf7ngy0y0" style="border: 0pt; width: 336px; height: 0px;"></div></div>
                                   </div></div></div></div></div></div>
            </div></div><small class="lbt lb-cm lpb-10 lpt-10 lpb-25 lmb-10 ddef had hdb">
                                     (Definition of <b>trunk</b> from the <a href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/" title="Cambridge Academic Content Dictionary" class="a--rev"><b>Cambridge Academic Content Dictionary</b></a> © Cambridge University Press)
                          </small></div>          
                  <div class="dataset dd pr lmb-20" data-type="sorted" data-id="examples" data-tab="ds-examples" role="tabpanel"><div class="cid" id="dataset_examples"></div>                         <div id="dataset-example" data-tab="ds-example" role="tabpanel">
                <div class="c_h">
              <h2 class="c_hh">Examples <span class="tcl">of </span><span class="tb tci">trunk</span></h2></div>

<div class="lp-10">
    <div class="h2 dhw dpos-h_hw  lpt-5 lpb-10">
        trunk
    </div>

    
    
    <div class="degs had lbt lb-cm">
        
                            <div class="lbb lb-cm lpt-10">

                                            <span class="deg">
                            In the <em>trunk</em> region, several signaling pathways and transcription factors have been implicated in cell fate decisions of neural crest.
                        </span>
                                        <div class="dsource lpr-20 pr">
                                    From the <a href="https://www.cambridge.org/gb/cambridgeenglish/better-learning-insights/corpus" class="italic" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cambridge English Corpus</a>                            <a class="report-example-inappropriate pa pt0 pr0" data-example-id="11855184" data-dataset-id="11666238" role="button" aria-label="Tell us what you think" title="Tell us what you think" tabindex="0" on="tap: formTellUs.clear,formTellUsDefinition.clear,AMP.setState({ stateSidebarEntryTellUs: { success: false, example_id: '11855184', dataset_id: '11666238' } }),sidebarEntryTellUs.open">
                            <i class="i i-comment fs14" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
                    
                            <div class="lbb lb-cm lpt-10">

                                            <span class="deg">
                            The arterial trunks both arose from the right ventricle with the aorta in right-sided position.
                        </span>
                                        <div class="dsource lpr-20 pr">
                                    From the <a href="https://www.cambridge.org/gb/cambridgeenglish/better-learning-insights/corpus" class="italic" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cambridge English Corpus</a>                            <a class="report-example-inappropriate pa pt0 pr0" data-example-id="11855185" data-dataset-id="11666238" role="button" aria-label="Tell us what you think" title="Tell us what you think" tabindex="0" on="tap: formTellUs.clear,formTellUsDefinition.clear,AMP.setState({ stateSidebarEntryTellUs: { success: false, example_id: '11855185', dataset_id: '11666238' } }),sidebarEntryTellUs.open">
                            <i class="i i-comment fs14" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
                    
                            <div class="lbb lb-cm lpt-10">

                                            <span class="deg">
                            When there is only one arterial <em>trunk</em>, and it gives rise to both systemic and pulmonary arteries, it is defined as a common arterial <em>trunk</em>.
                        </span>
                                        <div class="dsource lpr-20 pr">
                                    From the <a href="https://www.cambridge.org/gb/cambridgeenglish/better-learning-insights/corpus" class="italic" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cambridge English Corpus</a>                            <a class="report-example-inappropriate pa pt0 pr0" data-example-id="11855186" data-dataset-id="11666238" role="button" aria-label="Tell us what you think" title="Tell us what you think" tabindex="0" on="tap: formTellUs.clear,formTellUsDefinition.clear,AMP.setState({ stateSidebarEntryTellUs: { success: false, example_id: '11855186', dataset_id: '11666238' } }),sidebarEntryTellUs.open">
                            <i class="i i-comment fs14" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
                    
                            <div class="lbb lb-cm lpt-10">

                                            <span class="deg">
                            A solitary arterial <em>trunk</em> is described when it is not possible to define the connection of the pulmonary arteries to the heart.
                        </span>
                                        <div class="dsource lpr-20 pr">
                                    From the <a href="https://www.cambridge.org/gb/cambridgeenglish/better-learning-insights/corpus" class="italic" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cambridge English Corpus</a>                            <a class="report-example-inappropriate pa pt0 pr0" data-example-id="11855187" data-dataset-id="11666238" role="button" aria-label="Tell us what you think" title="Tell us what you think" tabindex="0" on="tap: formTellUs.clear,formTellUsDefinition.clear,AMP.setState({ stateSidebarEntryTellUs: { success: false, example_id: '11855187', dataset_id: '11666238' } }),sidebarEntryTellUs.open">
                            <i class="i i-comment fs14" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
                    
                            <div class="lbb lb-cm lpt-10">

                                            <span class="deg">
                            This roughly restores the circumference of the proximal pulmonary <em>trunk</em> bearing the two coronary arteries to what it was before developing the flaps.
                        </span>
                                        <div class="dsource lpr-20 pr">
                                    From the <a href="https://www.cambridge.org/gb/cambridgeenglish/better-learning-insights/corpus" class="italic" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cambridge English Corpus</a>                            <a class="report-example-inappropriate pa pt0 pr0" data-example-id="11855188" data-dataset-id="11666238" role="button" aria-label="Tell us what you think" title="Tell us what you think" tabindex="0" on="tap: formTellUs.clear,formTellUsDefinition.clear,AMP.setState({ stateSidebarEntryTellUs: { success: false, example_id: '11855188', dataset_id: '11666238' } }),sidebarEntryTellUs.open">
                            <i class="i i-comment fs14" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
                    
                            <div class="lbb lb-cm lpt-10">

                                            <span class="deg">
                            Patients with common arterial <em>trunk</em> with nonconfluent pulmonary arteries, however, were excluded.
                        </span>
                                        <div class="dsource lpr-20 pr">
                                    From the <a href="https://www.cambridge.org/gb/cambridgeenglish/better-learning-insights/corpus" class="italic" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cambridge English Corpus</a>                            <a class="report-example-inappropriate pa pt0 pr0" data-example-id="11855189" data-dataset-id="11666238" role="button" aria-label="Tell us what you think" title="Tell us what you think" tabindex="0" on="tap: formTellUs.clear,formTellUsDefinition.clear,AMP.setState({ stateSidebarEntryTellUs: { success: false, example_id: '11855189', dataset_id: '11666238' } }),sidebarEntryTellUs.open">
                            <i class="i i-comment fs14" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
                    
                            <div class="lbb lb-cm lpt-10">

                                            <span class="deg">
                            Both pulmonary arteries took theirorigins from the posterior aspect of this <em>trunk</em>.
                        </span>
                                        <div class="dsource lpr-20 pr">
                                    From the <a href="https://www.cambridge.org/gb/cambridgeenglish/better-learning-insights/corpus" class="italic" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cambridge English Corpus</a>                            <a class="report-example-inappropriate pa pt0 pr0" data-example-id="11855190" data-dataset-id="11666238" role="button" aria-label="Tell us what you think" title="Tell us what you think" tabindex="0" on="tap: formTellUs.clear,formTellUsDefinition.clear,AMP.setState({ stateSidebarEntryTellUs: { success: false, example_id: '11855190', dataset_id: '11666238' } }),sidebarEntryTellUs.open">
                            <i class="i i-comment fs14" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
                    
                            <div class="lbb lb-cm lpt-10">

                                            <span class="deg">
                            The distance between the posteriorly located pulmonary <em>trunk</em> and the aortic arch and descending aorta was shorter than in cases with concordant connections.
                        </span>
                                        <div class="dsource lpr-20 pr">
                                    From the <a href="https://www.cambridge.org/gb/cambridgeenglish/better-learning-insights/corpus" class="italic" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cambridge English Corpus</a>                            <a class="report-example-inappropriate pa pt0 pr0" data-example-id="11855191" data-dataset-id="11666238" role="button" aria-label="Tell us what you think" title="Tell us what you think" tabindex="0" on="tap: formTellUs.clear,formTellUsDefinition.clear,AMP.setState({ stateSidebarEntryTellUs: { success: false, example_id: '11855191', dataset_id: '11666238' } }),sidebarEntryTellUs.open">
                            <i class="i i-comment fs14" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
                    
                            <div class="lbb lb-cm lpt-10">

                                            <span class="deg">
                            This region led to the pulmonary <em>trunk</em> but the ventriculoarterial junction was unguarded.
                        </span>
                                        <div class="dsource lpr-20 pr">
                                    From the <a href="https://www.cambridge.org/gb/cambridgeenglish/better-learning-insights/corpus" class="italic" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cambridge English Corpus</a>                            <a class="report-example-inappropriate pa pt0 pr0" data-example-id="11855192" data-dataset-id="11666238" role="button" aria-label="Tell us what you think" title="Tell us what you think" tabindex="0" on="tap: formTellUs.clear,formTellUsDefinition.clear,AMP.setState({ stateSidebarEntryTellUs: { success: false, example_id: '11855192', dataset_id: '11666238' } }),sidebarEntryTellUs.open">
                            <i class="i i-comment fs14" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
                    
                            <div class="lbb lb-cm lpt-10">

                                            <span class="deg">
                            Leaves are shed at the end of the rainy or in the early dry season and trunks rehydrate.
                        </span>
                                        <div class="dsource lpr-20 pr">
                                    From the <a href="https://www.cambridge.org/gb/cambridgeenglish/better-learning-insights/corpus" class="italic" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cambridge English Corpus</a>                            <a class="report-example-inappropriate pa pt0 pr0" data-example-id="11855193" data-dataset-id="11666238" role="button" aria-label="Tell us what you think" title="Tell us what you think" tabindex="0" on="tap: formTellUs.clear,formTellUsDefinition.clear,AMP.setState({ stateSidebarEntryTellUs: { success: false, example_id: '11855193', dataset_id: '11666238' } }),sidebarEntryTellUs.open">
                            <i class="i i-comment fs14" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
                    
                            <div class="lbb lb-cm lpt-10">

                                            <span class="deg">
                            The pulmonary <em>trunk</em> was prominent in 15 (75%) and, in one patient with pulmonary atresia with ventricular septal defect, there was a rightsided aortic arch.
                        </span>
                                        <div class="dsource lpr-20 pr">
                                    From the <a href="https://www.cambridge.org/gb/cambridgeenglish/better-learning-insights/corpus" class="italic" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cambridge English Corpus</a>                            <a class="report-example-inappropriate pa pt0 pr0" data-example-id="11855194" data-dataset-id="11666238" role="button" aria-label="Tell us what you think" title="Tell us what you think" tabindex="0" on="tap: formTellUs.clear,formTellUsDefinition.clear,AMP.setState({ stateSidebarEntryTellUs: { success: false, example_id: '11855194', dataset_id: '11666238' } }),sidebarEntryTellUs.open">
                            <i class="i i-comment fs14" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
                    
                            <div class="lbb lb-cm lpt-10">

                                            <span class="deg">
                            The diameter of the pulmonary <em>trunk</em> diameter was measured at the midpoint between the pulmonary valve and the bifurcation.
                        </span>
                                        <div class="dsource lpr-20 pr">
                                    From the <a href="https://www.cambridge.org/gb/cambridgeenglish/better-learning-insights/corpus" class="italic" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cambridge English Corpus</a>                            <a class="report-example-inappropriate pa pt0 pr0" data-example-id="11855195" data-dataset-id="11666238" role="button" aria-label="Tell us what you think" title="Tell us what you think" tabindex="0" on="tap: formTellUs.clear,formTellUsDefinition.clear,AMP.setState({ stateSidebarEntryTellUs: { success: false, example_id: '11855195', dataset_id: '11666238' } }),sidebarEntryTellUs.open">
                            <i class="i i-comment fs14" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
                    
                            <div class="lbb lb-cm lpt-10">

                                            <span class="deg">
                            E: the ratio of the pitch moments is set as 0.7 for the <em>trunk</em> to shake largely front-to-back.
                        </span>
                                        <div class="dsource lpr-20 pr">
                                    From the <a href="https://www.cambridge.org/gb/cambridgeenglish/better-learning-insights/corpus" class="italic" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cambridge English Corpus</a>                            <a class="report-example-inappropriate pa pt0 pr0" data-example-id="11855196" data-dataset-id="11666238" role="button" aria-label="Tell us what you think" title="Tell us what you think" tabindex="0" on="tap: formTellUs.clear,formTellUsDefinition.clear,AMP.setState({ stateSidebarEntryTellUs: { success: false, example_id: '11855196', dataset_id: '11666238' } }),sidebarEntryTellUs.open">
                            <i class="i i-comment fs14" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
                    
                            <div class="lbb lb-cm lpt-10">

                                            <span class="deg">
                            We report a most unusual course of a left coronary artery originating from the pulmonary <em>trunk</em> 3 and discuss the clinical implications.
                        </span>
                                        <div class="dsource lpr-20 pr">
                                    From the <a href="https://www.cambridge.org/gb/cambridgeenglish/better-learning-insights/corpus" class="italic" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cambridge English Corpus</a>                            <a class="report-example-inappropriate pa pt0 pr0" data-example-id="11855197" data-dataset-id="11666238" role="button" aria-label="Tell us what you think" title="Tell us what you think" tabindex="0" on="tap: formTellUs.clear,formTellUsDefinition.clear,AMP.setState({ stateSidebarEntryTellUs: { success: false, example_id: '11855197', dataset_id: '11666238' } }),sidebarEntryTellUs.open">
                            <i class="i i-comment fs14" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
                    
                            <div class="lbb lb-cm lpt-10">

                                            <span class="deg">
                            Such changes of the walls of the arterial trunks, however, were not discovered in our patient.
                        </span>
                                        <div class="dsource lpr-20 pr">
                                    From the <a href="https://www.cambridge.org/gb/cambridgeenglish/better-learning-insights/corpus" class="italic" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cambridge English Corpus</a>                            <a class="report-example-inappropriate pa pt0 pr0" data-example-id="11855198" data-dataset-id="11666238" role="button" aria-label="Tell us what you think" title="Tell us what you think" tabindex="0" on="tap: formTellUs.clear,formTellUsDefinition.clear,AMP.setState({ stateSidebarEntryTellUs: { success: false, example_id: '11855198', dataset_id: '11666238' } }),sidebarEntryTellUs.open">
                            <i class="i i-comment fs14" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>

            sendGAEvent('Channelization', chanel, topic, {'nonInteraction':1});

    });

	var polarbyteConfig = {

        section:  "dictionary" ,

        type:  "english_entry"         }
    
              polarbyteConfig.productContext = {"id":"english/trunk","type":"MonoEngEntry"};

    window.assistantTracker = {
        sendEvent: function(event, metadata) {
            if (!window || !window.polarbytereach || !window.polarbytereach.trackPublisherCustomEvent) {
                console.error('Failed to call polarbytereach.trackPublisherCustomEvent, tracker is not available:', { event, metadata});
                return;
            }

            try {
                window.polarbytereach.trackPublisherCustomEvent(event, metadata);
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Failed to call polarbytereach.trackPublisherCustomEvent:', { error, event, metadata });
            }
        },

        getEvent: function(action, label, property, value) {
            return {category: 'AI_ASSISTANT', action: action, label: label, property: property, value: value};
        },

        eventHeaderBtnClick: function() {
            var event = this.getEvent('HEADER_BTN_CLICK');
            this.sendEvent(event, undefined);
        },

        eventBurgerMenuItemClick: function() {
            var event = this.getEvent('BURGER_MENU_ITEM_CLICK');
            this.sendEvent(event, undefined);
        },

        eventMobileStickyBtnClick: function() {
            var event = this.getEvent('MOBILE_STICKY_BTN_CLICK');
            this.sendEvent(event, undefined);
        }
    };
  </script>

<amp-state id="stateSidebarDict" class="i-amphtml-element i-amphtml-layout-container i-amphtml-built" i-amphtml-layout="container" hidden="" aria-hidden="true">
    <script type="application/json">
        {
        "open": false,
        "recent": true,
        "def": true,
        "gram": true,
        "trans": true,
        "pron": true,
        "plus": true
        }
    </script>
</amp-state>

<amp-sidebar id="sidebarDict" layout="nodisplay" side="right" class="bw cm-f i-amphtml-element i-amphtml-layout-nodisplay i-amphtml-overlay i-amphtml-scrollable i-amphtml-built" on="sidebarOpen:AMP.setState({ stateSidebarDict: { open: true } })" hidden="" i-amphtml-layout="nodisplay" role="menu" tabindex="-1">

    <div class="pr cm-fc cms lm-auto">
        <span class="pa pt0 pr0 lmt-5 lmr-15 lpt-15 hp fon" on="tap:sidebarDict.close" role="button" aria-label="Close" title="Close" tabindex="0">
            <i class="i i-close ibd" aria-hidden="true"></i>
        </span>

        <div class="han tc-bd fs14 lpt-5">

            <div class="fs18 lp-5 lpt-20 lpb-15 lpl-15">
                Choose a dictionary            </div>

            <ul class="hul-u hul-un hul-u0 lmb-0">
                <li class="lbt lb-cm">
                    <span class="hdb hax lp-10 lpl-15 lpr-15 hp" on="tap: AMP.setState({ stateSidebarDict: { recent: ! stateSidebarDict.recent } })">
                        <span class="pr hdb">
                            <span class="fs12 tcu">Recent and Recommended</span>
                            <i class="i i-minus ibd pa pr0 lpt-2" [class]="stateSidebarDict.recent ? 'i i-minus ibd pa pr0 lpt-2' : 'i i-plus ibd pa pr0 lpt-2'" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                        </span> 
                    </span>
                    <div class="lpl-15 lpr-15" [class]="stateSidebarDict.recent ? 'lpl-15 lpr-15' : 'hdn'">
                        <div class="pr tc-d fs16 lmb-20">
                            <div amp-access="1=1">
                                <div amp-access-template="">
                                        <span tabindex="0" aria-label="Set dictionary search to English" role="button" on="tap:AMP.setState({ stateSearch: { dataset: 'english', dataset_text: 'English', dataset_search: 'Search English', isbilang: false} }), sidebarDict.close, searchword.focus" class="hp hdb lmb-5 hp">
                                            English
                                        </span>
                                        <span tabindex="0" aria-label="Set dictionary search to English–Chinese (Traditional)" role="button" on="tap:AMP.setState({ stateSearch: { dataset: 'english-chinese-traditional', dataset_text: 'English–Chinese (Traditional)', dataset_search: 'Search English–Chinese (Traditional)', isbilang: false} }), sidebarDict.close, searchword.focus" class="hp hdb lmb-5 hp">
                                            English–Chinese (Traditional)
                                        </span>
                                        <span tabindex="0" aria-label="Set dictionary search to English–Chinese (Simplified)" role="button" on="tap:AMP.setState({ stateSearch: { dataset: 'english-chinese-simplified', dataset_text: 'English–Chinese (Simplified)', dataset_search: 'Search English–Chinese (Simplified)', isbilang: false} }), sidebarDict.close, searchword.focus" class="hp hdb lmb-5 hp">
                                            English–Chinese (Simplified)
                                        </span>
                                        <span tabindex="0" aria-label="Set dictionary search to Grammar" role="button" on="tap:AMP.setState({ stateSearch: { dataset: 'british-grammar', dataset_text: 'Grammar', dataset_search: 'Search Grammar', isbilang: false} }), sidebarDict.close, searchword.focus" class="hp hdb lmb-5 hp">
                                            Grammar
                                        </span>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div class="pa p0 bw" [class]="stateSidebarDict.open ? 'hdn' : 'pa p0 bw'">
                                <span class="pa p0 bload"></span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </li>

                                                <li class="lbt lb-cm">
                    <span class="hdb hax lp-10 lpl-15 lpr-15 hp" on="tap: AMP.setState({ stateSidebarDict: { def: ! stateSidebarDict.def } })">
                        <span class="pr hdb">
                            <span class="fs12 tcu">Definitions</span>
                            <i aria-hidden="true" class="i i-minus ibd pa pr0 lpt-2" [class]="stateSidebarDict.def ? 'i i-minus ibd pa pr0 lpt-2' : 'i i-plus ibd pa pr0 lpt-2'">
                            </i>
                        </span> 
                    </span>
                    <div class="lpl-15 lpr-15" [class]="stateSidebarDict.def ? 'lpl-15 lpr-15' : 'hdn'">
                        <div class="tc-bl lmb-5 lmt--3 break">
                            Clear explanations of natural written and spoken English                        </div>
                        <div class="hul-u tc-d fs16 hlh1_5 lmb-20">
                                                        <span data-dictcode="english" class="hp hdb hp" on="tap:AMP.setState({ stateSearch: { dataset: 'english', dataset_text: 'English', dataset_search: 'Search English', isbilang: false } }), sidebarDict.close, searchword.focus" role="button" aria-label="Set dictionary search to English" tabindex="0" title="English">English</span>
                                                        <span data-dictcode="learner-english" class="hp hdb hp" on="tap:AMP.setState({ stateSearch: { dataset: 'learner-english', dataset_text: 'Learner’s Dictionary', dataset_search: 'Search Learner’s Dictionary', isbilang: false } }), sidebarDict.close, searchword.focus" role="button" aria-label="Set dictionary search to Learner’s Dictionary" tabindex="0" title="Learner’s Dictionary">Learner’s Dictionary</span>
                                                        <span data-dictcode="essential-british-english" class="hp hdb hp" on="tap:AMP.setState({ stateSearch: { dataset: 'essential-british-english', dataset_text: 'Essential British English', dataset_search: 'Search Essential British English', isbilang: false } }), sidebarDict.close, searchword.focus" role="button" aria-label="Set dictionary search to Essential British English" tabindex="0" title="Essential British English">Essential British English</span>
                                                        <span data-dictcode="essential-american-english" class="hp hdb hp" on="tap:AMP.setState({ stateSearch: { dataset: 'essential-american-english', dataset_text: 'Essential American English', dataset_search: 'Search Essential American English', isbilang: false } }), sidebarDict.close, searchword.focus" role="button" aria-label="Set dictionary search to Essential American English" tabindex="0" title="Essential American English">Essential American English</span>
                                                </div>
                    </div>
                </li>
                
                                                <li class="lbt lb-cm">
                    <span class="hdb hax lp-10 lpl-15 lpr-15 hp" on="tap: AMP.setState({ stateSidebarDict: { gram: ! stateSidebarDict.gram } })">
                        <span class="pr hdb">
                            <span class="fs12 tcu">Grammar and thesaurus</span>
                            <i aria-hidden="true" class="i i-minus ibd pa pr0 lpt-2" [class]="stateSidebarDict.gram ? 'i i-minus ibd pa pr0 lpt-2' : 'i i-plus ibd pa pr0 lpt-2'">
                            </i>
                        </span> 
                    </span>
                    <div class="lpl-15 lpr-15" [class]="stateSidebarDict.gram ? 'lpl-15 lpr-15' : 'hdn'">
                        <div class="tc-bl lmb-5 lmt--3 break">
                            Usage explanations of natural written and spoken English                        </div>
                        <div class="hul-u tc-d fs16 hlh1_5 lmb-20">
                                                    <span data-dictcode="british-grammar" class="hp hdb" on="tap:AMP.setState({ stateSearch: { dataset: 'british-grammar', dataset_text: 'Grammar', dataset_search: 'Search Grammar', isbilang: false } }), sidebarDict.close, searchword.focus" role="button" aria-label="Set dictionary search to Grammar" tabindex="0" title="Grammar">Grammar</span>
                                                    <!-- need to rework this part when we have the spec for the thesaurus dataset selector -->
                                                        <span data-dictcode="thesaurus" class="hp" on="tap:AMP.setState({ stateSearch: { dataset: 'thesaurus', dataset_text: 'Thesaurus', dataset_search: 'Search Thesaurus', isbilang: false } }), sidebarDict.close, searchword.focus" role="button" aria-label="Set dictionary search to Thesaurus" tabindex="0" title="Thesaurus">Thesaurus</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </li>
                
                <li class="lbt lb-cm">
                    <span class="hdb hax lp-10 lpl-15 lpr-15 hp" on="tap: AMP.setState({ stateSidebarDict: { pron: ! stateSidebarDict.pron } })">
                        <span class="pr hdb">
                            <span class="fs12 tcu">Pronunciation</span>
                            <i aria-hidden="true" class="i i-minus ibd pa pr0 lpt-2" [class]="stateSidebarDict.pron ? 'i i-minus ibd pa pr0 lpt-2' : 'i i-plus ibd pa pr0 lpt-2'">
                            </i>
                        </span> 
                    </span>
                    <div class="lpl-15 lpr-15" [class]="stateSidebarDict.pron ? 'lpl-15 lpr-15' : 'hdn'">
                        <div class="tc-bl lmb-5 lmt--3 break">
